~~~text
工作目录：/home/ena/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init
// @exec: {"yield_time_ms": 1000}
const cwd="/home/ena/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init";
const feature="specs/docs/001-docs-site-framework";
const dest="xd4v3YLbHr6MFrshieoSPs";
const tmp=load("figmaT017Tmp");
const toolNames=new Set(["mcp__figma__search_design_system","mcp__figma__generate_figma_design","mcp__figma__get_screenshot"]);
for(const spec of ALL_TOOLS.filter(x=>toolNames.has(x.name))){
 const lead=spec.description.split("\n\nThe official Figma MCP server.")[0];
 const def=spec.description.split("exec tool declaration:").pop();
 text({name:spec.name,description:lead.slice(0,1600),schema:spec.description.includes("exec tool declaration:")?def.slice(0,3600):undefined});
}
const jobs=[
 ["Working tree and task",tools.exec_command({cmd:`git status --short --branch
SPECIFY_FEATURE_DIRECTORY=${feature} .specify/scripts/bash/check-prerequisites.sh --json --require-tasks --include-tasks
rg -c '^[-] \\[[xX]\\]' ${feature}/checklists/*.md
rg -n '^[-] \\[[x ]\\] T01[789] |^### 采用方式与 T019 输入' ${feature}/{tasks.md,analysis/figma-conversion.md}`,workdir:cwd,max_output_tokens:2500})],
 ["Figma session",tools.mcp__figma__create_session({fileKey:dest,description:"Run one four-view pilot from the actual Make preview into the approved Design page. Verify named, editable frames, layout, content and capture differences, then save the task evidence in the repository and report it in chat."})],
 ["Check preview inputs",tools.exec_command({cmd:`test -f source/dist/index.html && test -d browser/node_modules/playwright-core && sha256sum t018/capture.js && fc-list ':lang=zh' family | sort -u | head -8 && python3 -c 'import uuid; print(uuid.uuid4())'`,workdir:tmp,max_output_tokens:900})],
];
for(const [i,res] of(await Promise.allSettled(jobs.map(([,p])=>p))).entries()){
 if(i===1&&res.status==="fulfilled")store("t019Session",res.value);
 if(i===2&&res.status==="fulfilled"){
   const uuid=res.value.output.trim().split("\n").at(-1);
   if(/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/.test(uuid))store("t019PromptId",uuid);
 }
 text({label:jobs[i][0],result:res});
}

~~~

~~~text
# T019 实施方案

2026-11-27，用户在收到「按 T018 选定方式一次写入首页和技能集合页的桌面、手机四块画板，并验证可编辑性、裁切与可见状态」后回复「开始」，确认执行代表性转换并写入其提供的 Design 文件。 [T018 实测](../analysis/figma-conversion.md#t018-转换方式实测) · [原任务](../tasks.md#phase-8-user-story-6---一次启动将原型页面和状态转换为可编辑画板priority-p2) · [Design 目标页面](https://www.figma.com/design/xd4v3YLbHr6MFrshieoSPs/Untitled?node-id=0-1)

用 T017 核对过的 Make 源码启动本地预览。一次 Playwright 批次依次采集首页 Skills 章节和 frontend 集合页，桌面使用 1440 × 900，手机使用 390 × 844。首页两块取当前默认章节，集合桌面取默认侧栏，集合手机打开目录并保留 frontend 展开。各项确认标题、正文、视口和导航就绪后提交给 Code to canvas，四次采集指向同一文件的 `Page 1`。 [原型输入](https://www.figma.com/make/FOn29DUppcpuJ3bRP9QEOU/Agent-Foundry-%E6%96%87%E6%A1%A3%E7%AB%99) · [页面转换契约](../contracts/site-navigation.md#页面转换契约figma)

以任务清单中的实际视图和状态为画板命名，逐块对照浏览器参考画面与 Design 结果。核对常规文字是否为可编辑文本层、布局是否为可选和可调整的图层，以及正文、导航、裁切、字体、图片和 3D 的可见表现。将成功或失败、实际画板链接、差异与恢复条件追加到 `analysis/figma-conversion.md`。发生字体、图片替代时记录具体项及设计协作者的确认结果。 [需求与验收](../spec.md#user-story-6---一次启动将原型页面和状态转换为可编辑画板-p2)

~~~

const tmp=load("figmaT017Tmp");
const root="/home/ena/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init";
const server=`figma_pilot_port=$(python3 -c 'import socket; sock=socket.socket(); sock.bind(("127.0.0.1",0)); print(sock.getsockname()[1])') && printf 'T019_PREVIEW_PORT=%s\\n' "$figma_pilot_port" && exec mise exec node@24.22.2 pnpm@12.16.1 -- pnpm run preview --host 127.0.0.1 --port "$figma_pilot_port" --strictPort`;
const calls=[
 ["Design system lookup",tools.mcp__figma__search_design_system({fileKey,sessionId,queries:[{entity:"component",query:"Agent Foundry brand logo"},{entity:"style",query:"Instrument Serif chapter title"}]})],
 ["Design page baseline",tools.mcp__figma__get_metadata({fileKey,nodeId:"0:1",sessionId})],
 ["Preview startup",tools.exec_command({cmd:server,workdir:tmp+"/source",max_output_tokens:850,tty:true,yield_time_ms:1000})],
 ["Format and read back preparation",tools.exec_command({cmd:"pnpm exec eslint --fix --no-ignore --max-warnings 0 specs/docs/001-docs-site-framework/implementation/T019.md && cat specs/docs/001-docs-site-framework/implementation/T019.md",workdir:root,max_output_tokens:1800})]
];
for(const [i,res] of(await Promise.allSettled(calls.map(([,p])=>p))).entries()){
 if(res.status==="fulfilled"){
  store(["t019DesignLookup","t019Baseline","t019Preview","t019PlanCheck"][i],res.value);
  if(i===2){
    const port=res.value.output.match(/T019_PREVIEW_PORT=(\d+)/)?.[1];
    if(port)store("t019Port",Number(port));
  }
 }
 text({label:calls[i][0],result:i===0&&res.status==="fulfilled"?JSON.stringify(res.value).slice(0,2400):res});
}

      "text": "Capture ID generated: `4d9b7aae-dd59-4772-b3fd-ed661280bc69`\nTarget file: xd4v3YLbHr6MFrshieoSPs\n\n**CRITICAL — Capture IDs and Parallelism:**\n- Each capture ID is **single-use** and captures exactly **one page**. Never reuse a capture ID for multiple pages.\n- The hash URL containing the capture ID (e.g., `#figmacapture=...`) can only be submitted once. After submission, that URL becomes invalid and returns a 409 error if reused.\n- **Since the target file already exists, you CAN generate multiple capture IDs upfront and run them in parallel.** Call this tool once per page to get separate capture IDs, then open each page with its own capture ID simultaneously.\n- **Manual re-capture:** After a capture completes, the capture toolbar in the browser lets the user manually navigate to other pages and re-capture with automatically generated new capture IDs.\n\n## Instructions\n## Step 0: Choose Capture Mode (REQUIRED FIRST)\n\nClassify the target before doing anything else:\n\n- **EXTERNAL**: target URL host is NOT a local address (e.g., `https://example.com`).\n- **LOCAL**: target is served on `localhost`, `127.0.0.1`, `[::1]`, `0.0.0.0`, or `*.local`, or is a page from the current codebase.\n\nHard rules:\n- If EXTERNAL → go to **Step 1A (Playwright)**. Skip Step 1B entirely.\n- If LOCAL → go to **Step 1B (Script Tag)**. Skip Step 1A entirely.\n- Never run `open <external-url>#figmacapture=...` — it silently fails without the injected script.\n- `file://` URLs are not supported.\n- Do NOT download/curl external pages to serve locally — it will not work.\n\n## Step 1A: External Capture via Playwright\n\nUse this for any non-local URL (a site you do not control).\n\n**If Playwright MCP is not available in your tool list**, inform the user that capturing external sites requires the Playwright MCP server. Ask if they'd like you to install it. Do NOT attempt the script-tag method on external sites — it will silently fail.\n\n## Playwright MCP (for external sites)\n\nUse when you can't add the script to the page. Bypasses CSP restrictions:\n\n```javascript\nasync (page) => {\n  await page.route('**/*', async (route) => {\n    const response = await route.fetch();\n    const headers = { ...response.headers() };\n    delete headers['content-security-policy'];\n    delete headers['content-security-policy-report-only'];\n    await route.fulfill({ response, headers });\n  });\n  await page.goto('<TARGET_URL>');\n  const r = await page.context().request.get('https://mcp.figma.com/mcp/html-to-design/capture.js');\n  await page.evaluate((s) => { const el = document.createElement('script'); el.textContent = s; document.head.appendChild(el); }, await r.text());\n  await page.waitForTimeout(500);\n  return await page.evaluate(() => window.figma.captureForDesign({ captureId: '4d9b7aae-dd59-4772-b3fd-ed661280bc69', endpoint: 'https://mcp.figma.com/mcp/capture/4d9b7aae-dd59-4772-b3fd-ed661280bc69/submit?bindVariables=true&promptId=20586556-4cc7-4ab7-b823-b98e3a03e154', selector: 'body' }));\n}\n```\n\nAfter running the Playwright script, go to **Step 2: Poll Loop**.\n\n## Step 1B: Local Capture via Script Tag\n\nUse this for ALL local/dev projects where you can edit source code. Do NOT use Playwright for local projects.\n\n### Resolve Target Page From Codebase\n\nSearch the repository to determine:\n1. **Entry route/page** to capture (e.g., homepage `/`, a specific route like `/dashboard`).\n2. **File to edit** for script injection (e.g., `index.html`, `public/index.html`, `app/layout.tsx`).\n3. **Dev server command** (from package.json scripts or framework config).\n4. **Final local URL** (e.g., `http://localhost:5173/`).\n\nDo not ask the user for a URL until codebase discovery fails.\n\n### Dev Server Setup\n\nWhenever you need to start or use a local dev server:\n1. Check if a dev server is already running on the default port (e.g. 5173 for Vite, 3000 for Next.js, 4200 for Angular, 8080 for http-server). Use `lsof -i :<port>` on macOS/Linux or `netstat -ano | findstr :<port>` on Windows. If one is running and serving the right content, reuse it.\n2. If not running, start one using the project's dev command (e.g. `npm run dev`). For plain HTML files, use `npx http-server . -p 3000` or `python3 -m http.server 3000`.\n3. Verify the port is free before binding. If occupied, pick a different port.\n4. Use `http://localhost:<port>/...` as your target URL.\n\n### Add Capture Script and Open the Page\n\nTo capture the page, add this snippet to your HTML. It prepares the page for conversion to Figma:\n\n```html\n<script src=\"https://mcp.figma.com/mcp/html-to-design/capture.js\" async></script>\n```\n\nThe script automatically detects and captures the page structure when loaded.\n\nOpen this URL (replace `<target-url>` with the actual localhost URL):\n\n- **macOS**: `open \"<target-url>#figmacapture=4d9b7aae-dd59-4772-b3fd-ed661280bc69&figmaendpoint=https%3A%2F%2Fmcp.figma.com%2Fmcp%2Fcapture%2F4d9b7aae-dd59-4772-b3fd-ed661280bc69%2Fsubmit%3FbindVariables%3Dtrue%26promptId%3D20586556-4cc7-4ab7-b823-b98e3a03e154&figmadelay=1000\"`\n- **Linux**: `xdg-open \"<target-url>#figmacapture=4d9b7aae-dd59-4772-b3fd-ed661280bc69&figmaendpoint=https%3A%2F%2Fmcp.figma.com%2Fmcp%2Fcapture%2F4d9b7aae-dd59-4772-b3fd-ed661280bc69%2Fsubmit%3FbindVariables%3Dtrue%26promptId%3D20586556-4cc7-4ab7-b823-b98e3a03e154&figmadelay=1000\"`\n- **Windows**: `start \"<target-url>#figmacapture=4d9b7aae-dd59-4772-b3fd-ed661280bc69&figmaendpoint=https%3A%2F%2Fmcp.figma.com%2Fmcp%2Fcapture%2F4d9b7aae-dd59-4772-b3fd-ed661280bc69%2Fsubmit%3FbindVariables%3Dtrue%26promptId%3D20586556-4cc7-4ab7-b823-b98e3a03e154&figmadelay=1000\"`\n\nOptional: `&figmadelay=<ms>` (wait before capture), `&figmaselector=<css>` (capture specific element), `&figmaselector=*` (manual element selection UI)\n\nAfter opening, go to **Step 2: Poll Loop**.\n\n## Step 2: Poll Loop (MANDATORY)\n\n1. Wait 5 seconds.\n2. Call this tool again with BOTH `fileKey: \"xd4v3YLbHr6MFrshieoSPs\"` and `captureId: \"4d9b7aae-dd59-4772-b3fd-ed661280bc69\"` (fileKey is required on every call).\n3. If status is `pending` or `processing`, go back to step 1 and repeat.\n4. Stop only when status is `completed`.\n5. If still `pending` after 10 polls, troubleshoot (verify script injection, dev server, URL).\n\nNever switch methods because of `pending` or `processing`.\nNever create a new capture ID while polling this one.\nNever stop polling after only 1–2 attempts.\n\n**Now execute Steps 1–3 above.** Step 3 (polling) is critical — follow the loop exactly.\n\n## Capturing Additional Pages\n\n**Programmatic multi-page capture (recommended):** For each additional page, call this tool again with the SAME `fileKey`. Each call generates a new capture ID that adds to this same file. Use each capture ID's hash URL to open and capture its respective page.\n\n**Manual capture (alternative):** After this capture completes, the browser will show a capture toolbar. The user can manually click links to navigate to other pages, then use the toolbar to re-capture. The toolbar handles generating new capture IDs automatically.\n\n**Important:** Every page must be opened with the hash URL parameters (including the captureId) to load the capture script. Opening pages without the hash URL will not work.\n\n**Note:** The maximum capture size, including serialized images, is 50MB.\n\nSet up local font access for Figma Agent & MCP: https://help.figma.com/hc/en-us/articles/360039956894-Add-a-font-to-Figma.\nUpload custom fonts to your organization for Figma Agent & MCP: https://help.figma.com/hc/en-us/articles/360039956774-Upload-custom-fonts-to-an-organization."

~~~text

const root="/home/ena/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init";
const path="specs/docs/001-docs-site-framework/tasks.md";
const ref=await tools.exec_command({cmd:`rg '^[-] \\[ \\] T019 \\[US6\\] ' ${path}`,workdir:root,max_output_tokens:700});
const row=ref.output.trim();
if(ref.exit_code!==0||!row.includes("至少四块具名画板")||row.includes("\n")||row.includes("implementation/T019.md"))throw new Error("T019 preparation reference does not match original scope");
text(await tools.apply_patch(`*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: ${path}\n@@\n-${row}\n+${row} [实施方案](implementation/T019.md)\n*** End Patch`));
const lint=await tools.exec_command({cmd:`pnpm exec eslint --fix --no-ignore --max-warnings 0 ${path} && rg -n '^[-] \\[ \\] T019 ' ${path}`,workdir:root,max_output_tokens:850});
text(lint);
if(lint.exit_code!==0)exit();
const sessionId=load("t019Session").structuredContent.sessionId;
const plan={fileKey:"xd4v3YLbHr6MFrshieoSPs",nodeId:"0:1",sessionId,promptId:load("t019PromptId")};
store("t019CaptureArgs",plan);
const res=await tools.mcp__figma__generate_figma_design(plan);
store("t019CaptureFirst",res);
const clean=JSON.stringify(res).replace(/[0-9a-f]{8}(?:-[0-9a-f]{4}){3}-[0-9a-f]{12}/gi,"<UUID>");
text({firstCaptureInstructions:clean.slice(0,11500)});

~~~

After submission, that URL becomes invalid and returns a 409 error if reused.\n- **Since the target file already exists, you CAN generate multiple capture IDs upfront and run them in parallel.** Call this tool once per page to get separate capture IDs, then open each page with its own capture ID simultaneously.\n- **Manual re-capture:** After a capture completes, the capture toolbar in the browser lets the user manually navigate to other pages and re-capture with automatically generated new capture IDs.\n\n## Instructions\n## Step 0: Choose Capture Mode (REQUIRED FIRST)\n\nClassify the target before doing anything else:\n\n- **EXTERNAL**: target URL host is NOT a local address (e.g., `https://example.com`).\n- **LOCAL**: target is served on `localhost`, `127.0.0.1`, `[::1]`, `0.0.0.0`, or `*.local`, or is a page from the current codebase.\n\nHard rules:\n- If EXTERNAL → go to **Step 1A (Playwright)**. Skip Step 1B entirely.\n- If LOCAL → go to **Step 1B (Script Tag)**. Skip Step 1A entirely.\n- Never run `open <external-url>#figmacapture=...` — it silently fails without the injected script.\n- `file://` URLs are not supported.\n- Do NOT download/curl external pages to serve locally — it will not work.\n\n## Step 1A: External Capture via Playwright\n\nUse this for any non-local URL (a site you do not control).\n\n**If Playwright MCP is not available in your tool list**, inform the user that capturing external sites requires the Playwright MCP server. Ask if they'd like you to install it. Do NOT attempt the script-tag method on external sites — it will silently fail.\n\n## Playwright MCP (for external sites)\n\nUse when you can't add the script to the page. Bypasses CSP restrictions:\n\n```javascript\nasync (page) => {\n  await page.route('**/*', async (route) => {\n    const response = await route.fetch();\n    const headers = { ...response.headers() };\n    delete headers['content-security-policy'];\n    delete headers['content-security-policy-report-only'];\n    await route.fulfill({ response, headers });\n  });\n  await page.goto('<TARGET_URL>');\n  const r = await page.context().request.get('https://mcp.figma.com/mcp/html-to-design/capture.js');\n  await page.evaluate((s) => { const el = document.createElement('script'); el.textContent = s; document.head.appendChild(el); }, await r.text());\n  await page.waitForTimeout(500);\n  return await page.evaluate(() => window.figma.captureForDesign({ captureId: '7a76c5a8-7d67-4c3f-a7c3-472c15a6420e', endpoint: 'https://mcp.figma.com/mcp/capture/7a76c5a8-7d67-4c3f-a7c3-472c15a6420e/submit?bindVariables=true&promptId=20586556-4cc7-4ab7-b823-b98e3a03e154', selector: 'body' }));\n}\n```\n\nAfter running the Playwright script, go to **Step 2: Poll Loop**.\n\n## Step 1B: Local Capture via Script Tag\n\nUse this for ALL local/dev projects where you can edit source code. Do NOT use Playwright for local projects.\n\n### Resolve Target Page From Codebase\n\nSearch the repository to determine:\n1. **Entry route/page** to capture (e.g., homepage `/`, a specific route like `/dashboard`).\n2. **File to edit** for script injection (e.g., `index.html`, `public/index.html`, `app/layout.tsx`).\n3. **Dev server command** (from package.json scripts or framework config).\n4. **Final local URL** (e.g., `http://localhost:5173/`).\n\nDo not ask the user for a URL until codebase discovery fails.\n\n### Dev Server Setup\n\nWhenever you need to start or use a local dev server:\n1. Check if a dev server is already running on the default port (e.g. 5173 for Vite, 3000 for Next.js, 4200 for Angular, 8080 for http-server). Use `lsof -i :<port>` on macOS/Linux or `netstat -ano | findstr :<port>` on Windows. If one is running and serving the right content, reuse it.\n2. If not running, start one using the project's dev command (e.g. `npm run dev`). For plain HTML files, use `npx http-server . -p 3000` or `python3 -m http.server 3000`.\n3. Verify the port is free before binding. If occupied, pick a different port.\n4. Use `http://localhost:<port>/...` as your target URL.\n\n### Add Capture Script and Open the Page\n\nTo capture the page, add this snippet to your HTML. It prepares the page for conversion to Figma:\n\n```html\n<script src=\"https://mcp.figma.com/mcp/html-to-design/capture.js\" async></script>\n```\n\nThe script automatically detects and captures the page structure when loaded.\n\nOpen this URL (replace `<target-url>` with the actual localhost URL):\n\n- **macOS**: `open \"<target-url>#figmacapture=7a76c5a8-7d67-4c3f-a7c3-472c15a6420e&figmaendpoint=https%3A%2F%2Fmcp.figma.com%2Fmcp%2Fcapture%2F7a76c5a8-7d67-4c3f-a7c3-472c15a6420e%2Fsubmit%3FbindVariables%3Dtrue%26promptId%3D20586556-4cc7-4ab7-b823-b98e3a03e154&figmadelay=1000\"`\n- **Linux**: `xdg-open \"<target-url>#figmacapture=7a76c5a8-7d67-4c3f-a7c3-472c15a6420e&figmaendpoint=https%3A%2F%2Fmcp.figma.com%2Fmcp%2Fcapture%2F7a76c5a8-7d67-4c3f-a7c3-472c15a6420e%2Fsubmit%3FbindVariables%3Dtrue%26promptId%3D20586556-4cc7-4ab7-b823-b98e3a03e154&figmadelay=1000\"`\n- **Windows**: `start \"<target-url>#figmacapture=7a76c5a8-7d67-4c3f-a7c3-472c15a6420e&figmaendpoint=https%3A%2F%2Fmcp.figma.com%2Fmcp%2Fcapture%2F7a76c5a8-7d67-4c3f-a7c3-472c15a6420e%2Fsubmit%3FbindVariables%3Dtrue%26promptId%3D20586556-4cc7-4ab7-b823-b98e3a03e154&figmadelay=1000\"`\n\nOptional: `&figmadelay=<ms>` (wait before capture), `&figmaselector=<css>` (capture specific element), `&figmaselector=*` (manual element selection UI)\n\nAfter opening, go to **Step 2: Poll Loop**.\n\n## Step 2: Poll Loop (MANDATORY)\n\n1. Skip Step 1A entirely.\n- Never run `open <external-url>#figmacapture=...` — it silently fails without the injected script.\n- `file://` URLs are not supported.\n- Do NOT download/curl external pages to serve locally — it will not work.\n\n## Step 1A: External Capture via Playwright\n\nUse this for any non-local URL (a site you do not control).\n\n**If Playwright MCP is not available in your tool list**, inform the user that capturing external sites requires the Playwright MCP server. Ask if they'd like you to install it. Do NOT attempt the script-tag method on external sites — it will silently fail.\n\n## Playwright MCP (for external sites)\n\nUse when you can't add the script to the page. Bypasses CSP restrictions:\n\n```javascript\nasync (page) => {\n  await page.route('**/*', async (route) => {\n    const response = await route.fetch();\n    const headers = { ...response.headers() };\n    delete headers['content-security-policy'];\n    delete headers['content-security-policy-report-only'];\n    await route.fulfill({ response, headers });\n  });\n  await page.goto('<TARGET_URL>');\n  const r = await page.context().request.get('https://mcp.figma.com/mcp/html-to-design/capture.js');\n  await page.evaluate((s) => { const el = document.createElement('script'); el.textContent = s; document.head.appendChild(el); }, await r.text());\n  await page.waitForTimeout(500);\n  return await page.evaluate(() => window.figma.captureForDesign({ captureId: '4c0ffdbd-85db-43be-897e-36307837ad82', endpoint: 'https://mcp.figma.com/mcp/capture/4c0ffdbd-85db-43be-897e-36307837ad82/submit?bindVariables=true&promptId=20586556-4cc7-4ab7-b823-b98e3a03e154', selector: 'body' }));\n}\n```\n\nAfter running the Playwright script, go to **Step 2: Poll Loop**.\n\n## Step 1B: Local Capture via Script Tag\n\nUse this for ALL local/dev projects where you can edit source code. Do NOT use Playwright for local projects.\n\n### Resolve Target Page From Codebase\n\nSearch the repository to determine:\n1. **Entry route/page** to capture (e.g., homepage `/`, a specific route like `/dashboard`).\n2. **File to edit** for script injection (e.g., `index.html`, `public/index.html`, `app/layout.tsx`).\n3. **Dev server command** (from package.json scripts or framework config).\n4. **Final local URL** (e.g., `http://localhost:5173/`).\n\nDo not ask the user for a URL until codebase discovery fails.\n\n### Dev Server Setup\n\nWhenever you need to start or use a local dev server:\n1. Check if a dev server is already running on the default port (e.g. 5173 for Vite, 3000 for Next.js, 4200 for Angular, 8080 for http-server). Use `lsof -i :<port>` on macOS/Linux or `netstat -ano | findstr :<port>` on Windows. If one is running and serving the right content, reuse it.\n2. If not running, start one using the project's dev command (e.g. `npm run dev`). For plain HTML files, use `npx http-server . -p 3000` or `python3 -m http.server 3000`.\n3. Verify the port is free before binding. If occupied, pick a different port.\n4. Use `http://localhost:<port>/...` as your target URL.\n\n### Add Capture Script and Open the Page\n\nTo capture the page, add this snippet to your HTML. It prepares the page for conversion to Figma:\n\n```html\n<script src=\"https://mcp.figma.com/mcp/html-to-design/capture.js\" async></script>\n```\n\nThe script automatically detects and captures the page structure when loaded.\n\nOpen this URL (replace `<target-url>` with the actual localhost URL):\n\n- **macOS**: `open \"<target-url>#figmacapture=4c0ffdbd-85db-43be-897e-36307837ad82&figmaendpoint=https%3A%2F%2Fmcp.figma.com%2Fmcp%2Fcapture%2F4c0ffdbd-85db-43be-897e-36307837ad82%2Fsubmit%3FbindVariables%3Dtrue%26promptId%3D20586556-4cc7-4ab7-b823-b98e3a03e154&figmadelay=1000\"`\n- **Linux**: `xdg-open \"<target-url>#figmacapture=4c0ffdbd-85db-43be-897e-36307837ad82&figmaendpoint=https%3A%2F%2Fmcp.figma.com%2Fmcp%2Fcapture%2F4c0ffdbd-85db-43be-897e-36307837ad82%2Fsubmit%3FbindVariables%3Dtrue%26promptId%3D20586556-4cc7-4ab7-b823-b98e3a03e154&figmadelay=1000\"`\n- **Windows**: `start \"<target-url>#figmacapture=4c0ffdbd-85db-43be-897e-36307837ad82&figmaendpoint=https%3A%2F%2Fmcp.figma.com%2Fmcp%2Fcapture%2F4c0ffdbd-85db-43be-897e-36307837ad82%2Fsubmit%3FbindVariables%3Dtrue%26promptId%3D20586556-4cc7-4ab7-b823-b98e3a03e154&figmadelay=1000\"`\n\nOptional: `&figmadelay=<ms>` (wait before capture), `&figmaselector=<css>` (capture specific element), `&figmaselector=*` (manual element selection UI)\n\nAfter opening, go to **Step 2: Poll Loop**.\n\n## Step 2: Poll Loop (MANDATORY)\n\n1. Wait 5 seconds.\n2. Call this tool again with BOTH `fileKey: \"xd4v3YLbHr6MFrshieoSPs\"` and `captureId: \"4c0ffdbd-85db-43be-897e-36307837ad82\"` (fileKey is required on every call).\n3. If status is `pending` or `processing`, go back to step 1 and repeat.\n4. Stop only when status is `completed`.\n5. If still `pending` after 10 polls, troubleshoot (verify script injection, dev server, URL).\n\nNever switch methods because of `pending` or `processing`.\nNever create a new capture ID while polling this one.\nNever stop polling after only 1–2 attempts.\n\n**Now execute Steps 1–3 above.** Step 3 (polling) is critical — follow the loop exactly.\n\n## Capturing Additional Pages\n\n**Programmatic multi-page capture (recommended):** For each additional page, call this tool again with the SAME `fileKey`. Each call generates a new capture ID that adds to this same file. Use each capture ID's hash URL to open and capture its respective page.\n\n**Manual capture (alternative):** After this capture completes, the browser will show a capture toolbar. The user can manually click links to navigate to other pages, then use the toolbar to re-capture. The toolbar handles generating new capture IDs automatically.\n\n**Important:** Every page must be opened with the hash URL parameters (including the captureId) to load the capture script. Opening pages without the hash URL will not work.\n\n**Note:** The maximum capture size, including serialized images, is 50MB.\n\nSet up local font access for Figma Agent & MCP: https://help.figma.com/hc/en-us/articles/360039956894-Add-a-font-to-Figma.\nUpload custom fonts to your organization for Figma Agent & MCP: https://help.figma.com/hc/en-us/articles/360039956774-Upload-custom-fonts-to-an-organization."
    }
  ],
  "_meta": {
    "mcpRequestId": "47b7c02d-d573-4474-a086-2f61d3aa636f"
  }
}

const args=load("t019CaptureArgs");
const origin=`http://127.0.0.1:${load("t019Port")}`;
const cases=[
 {key:"home-desktop",name:"Skills 章节 · 桌面 · 默认",path:"/",width:1440,height:900,mobile:false},
 {key:"home-mobile",name:"Skills 章节 · 手机 · 默认",path:"/",width:390,height:844,mobile:true},
 {key:"frontend-desktop",name:"frontend 集合 · 桌面 · 默认",path:"/skills/frontend",width:1440,height:900,mobile:false},
 {key:"frontend-mobile",name:"frontend 集合 · 手机 · 目录仅展开 frontend",path:"/skills/frontend",width:390,height:844,mobile:true,openDirectory:true}
];
store("t019CaseSchema",cases);
const captures=[];
for(const [index,item] of cases.entries()) {
 let result;
 try {
   result=index===0?load("t019CaptureFirst"):await tools.mcp__figma__generate_figma_design(args);
 } catch(error) {captures.push({...item,error:String(error)});continue;}
 const body=(result.content??[]).filter(x=>x.type==="text").map(x=>x.text).join("\n");
 const captureId=body.match(/Capture ID generated:\s*`([0-9a-f-]{36})`/i)?.[1];
 const endpoint=body.match(/endpoint:\s*'(https:\/\/mcp\.figma\.com\/mcp\/capture\/[^']+)'/)?.[1];
 if(!captureId||!endpoint||!endpoint.includes(captureId)) {
   captures.push({...item,error:body.slice(0,1200)});
   continue;
 }
 captures.push({...item,captureId,endpoint});
}
store("t019Captures",captures);
text({origin,prepared:captures.map(({key,name,width,height,path,error})=>({key,name,width,height,path,error})),ready:captures.filter(x=>x.captureId).length,unique:new Set(captures.map(x=>x.captureId)).size});

## 工具调用 `exec` · `call_b6850cf57fc34946b58c89fc106737b4`

~~~text
工作目录：/home/ena/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init
const tmp=load("figmaT017Tmp");
const origin=`http://127.0.0.1:${load("t019Port")}`;
const captures=load("t019Captures");
if(captures?.length!==4||captures.some(x=>!x.captureId||!x.endpoint))throw new Error("Four authorized capture IDs are required");
const program=String.raw`import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {createHash} from "node:crypto";
import {writeFile} from "node:fs/promises";
import {chromium} from "playwright-core";

const [origin,outputPath,captureScript,input]=process.argv.slice(1);
const cases=JSON.parse(input);
const executablePath="/home/ena/.cache/ms-playwright/chromium_headless_shell-1248/chrome-headless-shell-linux64/chrome-headless-shell";
const browser=await chromium.launch({executablePath,headless:true,args:["--use-angle=swiftshader","--enable-unsafe-swiftshader"]});

const cleanupError=error=>String(error?.message??error).replace(/https:\/\/mcp\.figma\.com\/mcp\/capture\/[^\s"']+/g,"[Figma capture endpoint]").slice(0,550);

async function renderedFonts(page, selectors){
 const result={};
 try{
  const cdp=await page.context().newCDPSession(page);
  const dom=await cdp.send("DOM.getDocument");
  await cdp.send("DOM.enable");
  await cdp.send("CSS.enable");
  for(const selector of selectors){
   const node=await cdp.send("DOM.querySelector",{nodeId:dom.root.nodeId,selector});
   if(!node.nodeId) continue;
   const {fonts}=await cdp.send("CSS.getPlatformFontsForNode",{nodeId:node.nodeId});
   result[selector]=fonts.map(({familyName,postScriptName,glyphCount})=>({familyName,postScriptName,glyphCount}));
  }
  await cdp.detach();
 }catch(error){result.error=cleanupError(error);}
 return result;
}

async function prepareCase(page,item){
 let ready=await page.goto(origin+item.path,{waitUntil:"commit",timeout:20000});
 assert.equal(ready.status(),200);
 assert.equal(new URL(page.url()).pathname,item.path);
 await page.locator("main h1").first().waitFor({state:"attached",timeout:40000});
 if(item.path==="/"){
  await page.locator("#chapter-skills").waitFor({timeout:20000});
  await page.waitForFunction(()=>{
    const el=document.querySelector("#chapter-skills");
    const box=el?.getBoundingClientRect();
    return box && box.top>=0 && box.bottom<=innerHeight && el.closest("section").dataset.revealed==="true";
  },null,{polling:150,timeout:20000});
  await page.waitForFunction(()=>{
    const caption=document.querySelector('section[data-index="0"] figcaption')?.textContent??"";
    return caption.includes("实时渲染")||caption.includes("静态预览");
  },null,{polling:200,timeout:28000});
 }else{
  assert.equal((await page.locator("main h1").textContent()).trim(),"frontend");
  assert.ok((await page.locator("main").textContent()).includes("界面构建与前端工程相关的技能"));
  if(item.openDirectory){
    await page.getByRole("button",{name:"目录",exact:true}).tap();
    await page.locator("#skills-drawer").getByRole("link",{name:"技能文档总览",exact:true}).waitFor();
    await page.waitForFunction(()=>{
      const drawer=document.getElementById("skills-drawer");
      return drawer?.getBoundingClientRect().x===0 && getComputedStyle(drawer).visibility==="visible";
    },null,{polling:150,timeout:15000});
    assert.ok(await page.locator("#skills-drawer").getByRole("link",{name:"component-scaffold",exact:true}).isVisible());
  }else{
    assert.equal(await page.locator('aside[aria-label="技能目录"] button[aria-controls="aside-frontend"]').getAttribute("aria-expanded"),"true");
  }
 }
 const fontsReady=await page.evaluate(()=>Promise.race([document.fonts.ready.then(()=>true),new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(()=>resolve(false),12000))]));
 const selectors=item.path==="/"?["#chapter-skills",'section[data-index="0"] p.reveal','header a[aria-label="Agent Foundry 首页"]'] : ["main h1","main p","header a"];
 const fonts=await renderedFonts(page,selectors);
 return {
  fontsReady,fonts,
  heading:item.path==="/"?await page.locator("#chapter-skills").innerText():await page.getByRole("heading",{level:1}).innerText(),
  caption:item.path==="/"?await page.locator('section[data-index="0"] figcaption').innerText():null,
  chapterBox:item.path==="/"?await page.locator("#chapter-skills").boundingBox():null,
  drawer:item.openDirectory?{visibility:await page.locator("#skills-drawer").isVisible(),box:await page.locator("#skills-drawer").boundingBox()}:null
 };
}

const results=[];
try{
 for(const item of cases){
  let submitted=false;
  const failures=[];
  let result;
  for(let attempt=1;attempt<=2&&!submitted&&!result;attempt++){
    const context=await browser.newContext({viewport:{width:item.width,height:item.height},deviceScaleFactor:1,isMobile:item.mobile,hasTouch:item.mobile});
    const page=await context.newPage();
    page.setDefaultTimeout(17000);
    const requestTrouble=[];
    page.on("requestfailed",r=>{if(r.url()===item.endpoint)requestTrouble.push(r.failure()?.errorText??"POST failed");});
    try{
     const source=await prepareCase(page,item);
     const image=await page.screenshot({path:outputPath+"/"+item.key+"-browser.png",scale:"css",timeout:25000});
     const sourceRecord={source,referenceSha256:createHash("sha256").update(image).digest("hex")};
     await page.addScriptTag({path:captureScript});
     assert.equal(await page.evaluate(()=>typeof window.figma?.captureOnceForDesign),"function");
     console.log(JSON.stringify({ready:item.key,view:item.name,caption:source.caption?.replace(/\n/g," / "),fontsReady:source.fontsReady}));
     submitted=true;
     const waitForSend=page.waitForResponse(r=>r.url()===item.endpoint&&r.request().method()==="POST",{timeout:65000});
     const [send,execution]=await Promise.allSettled([
       waitForSend,
       page.evaluate(async({captureId,endpoint})=>{
         await window.figma.captureOnceForDesign({captureId,endpoint,selector:"body"});
       },{captureId:item.captureId,endpoint:item.endpoint})
     ]);
     result={key:item.key,view:item.name,width:item.width,height:item.height,submitted,
       http:send.status==="fulfilled"?send.value.status():null,
       bytes:send.status==="fulfilled"?send.value.request().postDataBuffer()?.length??null:null,
       scriptError:execution.status==="rejected"?cleanupError(execution.reason):null,requestTrouble,attempt,failures,...sourceRecord};
    }catch(error){
     failures.push(cleanupError(error));
     if(submitted)result={key:item.key,view:item.name,submitted,failures,requestTrouble};
    }finally{await context.close();}
  }
  result??={key:item.key,view:item.name,submitted,failures};
  results.push(result);
  console.log(JSON.stringify({case:result}));
  await writeFile(outputPath+"/browser-pilot.json",JSON.stringify(results,null,2));
 }
 assert.equal(results.length,4);
 console.log(JSON.stringify({browser:browser.version(),total:results.length,submitted:results.filter(x=>x.submitted).length,successfulResponses:results.filter(x=>x.http>=200&&x.http<300).length}));
}finally{await browser.close();}`;
store("t019BrowserProgram",program);
const q=s=>"'"+s.replace(/'/g,"'\\''")+"'";
const proc=await tools.exec_command({cmd:`mise exec node@24.22.2 -- node --input-type=module -e ${q(program)} ${q(origin)} ${q(tmp+"/t019")} ${q(tmp+"/t018/capture.js")} ${q(JSON.stringify(captures))}`,workdir:tmp+"/browser",max_output_tokens:8500,yield_time_ms:1500});
store("t019BrowserProcess",proc);
text(proc);

~~~

const root=load("figmaT017Tmp")+"/t019";
const response=load("t019FirstScreenshot");
const head=JSON.parse(response.content.find(x=>x.type==="text").text);
const url=head.image_url;
if(!url.startsWith("https://"))throw new Error("Unexpected screenshot URL");
const q=s=>"'"+s.replace(/'/g,"'\\''")+"'";
const fetch=await tools.exec_command({cmd:`curl --fail --silent --show-error --location --max-time 25 --output home-desktop-figma.png ${q(url)}`,workdir:root,max_output_tokens:300});
if(fetch.exit_code!==0) {text(fetch);exit();}
const paths=["home-desktop-browser.png","home-desktop-figma.png"];
const shot=await Promise.allSettled(paths.map(name=>tools.view_image({path:root+"/"+name})));
for(const [i,r] of shot.entries()){
 text(paths[i]);
 if(r.status==="fulfilled")image(r.value.image_url);
 else text(String(r.reason));
}

~~~

- 来源定位：字节 4980247

~~~text
工作目录：/home/ena/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init
// @exec: {"yield_time_ms": 1000}
const args=load("t019CaptureArgs");
const cases=load("t019Captures");
const seen=[];
const responses=await Promise.allSettled(cases.map(item=>{
 const old=load("t019Poll-"+item.key);
 return old?Promise.resolve(old):tools.mcp__figma__generate_figma_design({...args,captureId:item.captureId});
}));
for(const [i,r] of responses.entries()){
 const item=cases[i];
 if(r.status==="fulfilled"){
   store("t019Poll-"+item.key,r.value);
   const body=(r.value.content??[]).filter(x=>x.type==="text").map(x=>x.text).join("\n");
   const node=body.match(/https:\/\/www\.figma\.com\/design\/[^?\s]+\?node-id=(\d+-\d+)/)?.[1];
   seen.push({key:item.key,name:item.name,nodeId:node?.replace("-",":"),status:node?"completed":body.slice(0,200).replace(/[0-9a-f]{8}(?:-[0-9a-f]{4}){3}-[0-9a-f]{12}/gi,"<UUID>")});
 }else seen.push({key:item.key,error:String(r.reason)});
}
store("t019Nodes",seen);
text(seen);

~~~

~~~text
工作目录：/home/ena/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init
// @exec: {"yield_time_ms": 1000}
const dest="xd4v3YLbHr6MFrshieoSPs";
const sessionId=load("t019Session").structuredContent.sessionId;
const nodes=load("t019Nodes");
const inspect=String.raw`const expected=__NODES__;
const page=await figma.getNodeByIdAsync("0:1");
const report=[];
function bounds(node,base) {
 const b=node.absoluteBoundingBox;
 if(!b)return null;
 const p=base.absoluteBoundingBox;
 return {x:Math.round((b.x-p.x)*10)/10,y:Math.round((b.y-p.y)*10)/10,w:Math.round(b.width*10)/10,h:Math.round(b.height*10)/10};
}
function effectiveVisible(node, root) {
 const box=node.absoluteBoundingBox;
 let parent=node;
 while(parent){
    if(parent.visible===false||parent.opacity===0) return false;
    if(parent.clipsContent&&parent.absoluteBoundingBox&&box){
       const clip=parent.absoluteBoundingBox;
       if(box.x+box.width<=clip.x||box.y+box.height<=clip.y||box.x>=clip.x+clip.width||box.y>=clip.y+clip.height)return false;
    }
    if(parent.id===root.id)break;
    parent=parent.parent;
 }
 return true;
}
for(const item of expected){
 const root=await figma.getNodeByIdAsync(item.nodeId);
 if(!root||typeof root.findAll!=="function"){report.push({key:item.key,missing:true});continue;}
 const descendants=root.findAll(()=>true);
 const texts=descendants.filter(x=>x.type==="TEXT");
 const fonts={};
 const samples=[];
 const markers=["Skills","MCP","Agent Foundry","技能包","读取一项技能","把一类任务","立体文件夹","实时渲染","技能文档总览","frontend","界面构建与前端工程相关的技能","component-scaffold","workflow","文档待补充"];
 for(const node of texts){
   const ranges=node.getStyledTextSegments(["fontName"]);
   const families=Array.from(new Set(ranges.map(r=>r.fontName.family+" / "+r.fontName.style)));
   for(const one of families) fonts[one]=(fonts[one]??0)+1;
   const label=node.characters.trim();
   if(markers.some(m=>label===m||label.startsWith(m))&&samples.length<28) samples.push({id:node.id,text:label.slice(0,70),fonts:families,missing:node.hasMissingFont,visible:effectiveVisible(node,root),box:bounds(node,root)});
 }
 const imageNodes=descendants.filter(node=>Array.isArray(node.fills)&&node.fills.some(f=>f.type==="IMAGE"&&f.visible!==false));
 const imageDetails=imageNodes.slice(0,12).map(node=>({id:node.id,name:node.name,type:node.type,visible:effectiveVisible(node,root),box:bounds(node,root)}));
 const layoutTypes={};
 for(const n of descendants) if(n.type!=="TEXT")layoutTypes[n.type]=(layoutTypes[n.type]??0)+1;
 let cursor=root;
 const parents=[];
 while(cursor.parent&&cursor.type!=="PAGE"){parents.push(cursor.parent.id);cursor=cursor.parent;}
 report.push({key:item.key,nodeId:root.id,name:root.name,type:root.type,parent:root.parent?.id??null,parents,width:root.width,height:root.height,clipsContent:root.clipsContent,layoutMode:root.layoutMode,childCount:root.children?.length??0,texts:texts.length,missingFonts:texts.filter(n=>n.hasMissingFont).length,fonts,samples,imageDetails,layoutTypes});
}
return {page:{id:page?.id,name:page?.name,children:page?.children.map(x=>({id:x.id,type:x.type,name:x.name}))},report};`.replace("__NODES__",JSON.stringify(nodes));
const screenshotCalls=nodes.slice(1).map(x=>[x.key,tools.mcp__figma__get_screenshot({fileKey:dest,nodeId:x.nodeId,sessionId,maxDimension:1170})]);
const calls=[
 ["Layer audit",tools.mcp__figma__use_figma({fileKey:dest,sessionId,skillNames:"resource:figma-use",description:"Inspect the four imported frames, native text and layout layer counts, font families, visibility and image layers.",code:inspect})],
 ["Check first image download",tools.write_stdin({session_id:load("t019ScreenshotDownload").session_id,chars:"",yield_time_ms:5000,max_output_tokens:400})],
 ...screenshotCalls
];
for(const [i,r] of(await Promise.allSettled(calls.map(([,p])=>p))).entries()){
 if(r.status!=="fulfilled"){text({label:calls[i][0],error:String(r.reason)});continue;}
 if(i===0) {
  store("t019LayerAudit",r.value);
  const raw=(r.value.content??[]).find(x=>x.type==="text")?.text;
  try {
    const info=JSON.parse(raw);
    text({label:calls[i][0],page:info.page,frames:info.report.map(frame=>({...frame,samples:frame.samples.filter(x=>["Skills","MCP","Agent Foundry","frontend","技能文档总览","把一类任务"].some(t=>x.text.startsWith(t))).slice(0,10),imageDetails:frame.imageDetails.slice(0,4)}))});
  } catch {text({label:calls[i][0],raw:raw?.slice(0,3500),error:r.value.isError??false});}
 } else if(i===1){
   store("t019ScreenshotDownload",r.value);
   text({label:calls[i][0],result:r.value});
 } else {
   store("t019Screen-"+calls[i][0],r.value);
   const content=(r.value.content??[]).map(x=>x.type==="text"?x.text.replace(/https?:\/\/[^\s"'`)\]>]+/g,"[Figma image URL]").slice(0,170):x.type);
   text({label:calls[i][0],isError:r.value.isError??false,content});
 }
}

 if(!node||node.type!=="FRAME"||node.parent?.id!=="0:1"||node.width!==item.width)throw new Error("Unexpected frame "+item.nodeId);
 if(node.height!==item.height&&!(item.key==="frontend-mobile"&&node.height===861))throw new Error("Unexpected height at "+item.nodeId);

}

 const oldHeight=node.height;
 node.name=item.name;
 if(oldHeight!==item.height){
   node.primaryAxisSizingMode="FIXED";
   node.resize(item.width,item.height);
   node.clipsContent=true;
 }
 const brand=node.findOne(n=>n.type==="TEXT"&&n.characters.trim()==="Agent Foundry");
 const brands=[];
 let cursor=brand;
 while(cursor&&cursor.id!==node.id&&brands.length<8){
   brands.push({id:cursor.id,name:cursor.name,type:cursor.type,x:cursor.x,y:cursor.y,width:cursor.width,height:cursor.height,layoutMode:cursor.layoutMode,layoutPositioning:cursor.layoutPositioning,layoutSizingHorizontal:cursor.layoutSizingHorizontal,children:"children" in cursor?cursor.children.map(n=>({id:n.id,name:n.name,x:n.x,width:n.width})).slice(0,4):undefined});
   cursor=cursor.parent;
 }
 const imgs=[];
 if(item.key.startsWith("home"))for(const n of node.findAll(n=>Array.isArray(n.fills)&&n.fills.some(f=>f.type==="IMAGE"))){
   const fill=n.fills.find(f=>f.type==="IMAGE");
   const img=fill.imageHash&&figma.getImageByHash(fill.imageHash);
   imgs.push({id:n.id,name:n.name,width:n.width,height:n.height,blendMode:n.blendMode,opacity:n.opacity,imageSize:img?await img.getSizeAsync():null,scaleMode:fill.scaleMode,scalingFactor:fill.scalingFactor,imageTransform:fill.imageTransform,imageOpacity:fill.opacity});
 }
 report.push({key:item.key,id:node.id,name:node.name,width:node.width,height:node.height,previousHeight:oldHeight,clipsContent:node.clipsContent,brands,imgs});
}
return {mutatedNodeIds:frames.map(x=>x.node.id),report};`.replace("__FRAMES__",JSON.stringify(bindings.map(x=>({...x,width:x.key.endsWith("desktop")?1440:390,height:x.key.endsWith("desktop")?900:844}))));

  "description": "Restore the captured desktop header to the source maximum-width inset and set the original 180-pixel paper grain images to repeating tiles.",
  "code": "const rowIds=[\"3:38\",\"5:39\"];\nconst grainSpecs=[[\"3:43\",\"MULTIPLY\"],[\"4:30\",\"MULTIPLY\"],[\"3:164\",\"SCREEN\"],[\"4:134\",\"SCREEN\"]];\nconst rows=[];\nconst paints=[];\nfor(const id of rowIds){\n  const node=await figma.getNodeByIdAsync(id);\n  if(!node||node.type!==\"FRAME\"||node.layoutMode!==\"HORIZONTAL\"||node.width!==1440||![32,112].includes(node.paddingLeft)||![32,112].includes(node.paddingRight))throw new Error(\"Header no longer matches source shape \"+id);\n  rows.push(node);\n}\nfor(const [id,blendMode] of grainSpecs){\n  const node=await figma.getNodeByIdAsync(id);\n  if(!node||node.type!==\"FRAME\"||!Array.isArray(node.fills)||node.fills.length!==1||node.fills[0].type!==\"IMAGE\")throw new Error(\"Missing source grain layer \"+id);\n  const fill=node.fills[0];\n  const original=fill.imageHash&&figma.getImageByHash(fill.imageHash);\n  const size=original?await original.getSizeAsync():null;\n  if(size?.width!==180||size?.height!==180)throw new Error(\"Unexpected tile size \"+id);\n  const {imageTransform,...unchanged}=fill;\n  paints.push({node,blendMode,tile:{...unchanged,scaleMode:\"TILE\",scalingFactor:1}});\n}\nfor(const node of rows){node.paddingLeft=112;node.paddingRight=112;}\nfor(const {node,blendMode,tile} of paints){node.fills=[tile];node.blendMode=blendMode;}\nreturn {mutatedNodeIds:[...rows.map(n=>n.id),...paints.map(t=>t.node.id)],rows:rows.map(n=>({id:n.id,paddingLeft:n.paddingLeft,paddingRight:n.paddingRight,children:n.children.map(c=>({name:c.name,x:c.x}))})),grain:paints.map(({node})=>({id:node.id,blendMode:node.blendMode,scale:node.fills[0].scaleMode,factor:node.fills[0].scalingFactor,opacity:node.opacity}))};"

const grainSpecs=[["3:43","MULTIPLY"],["4:30","MULTIPLY"],["3:164","SCREEN"],["4:134","SCREEN"]];
const rows=[];
const paints=[];
for(const id of rowIds){
  const node=await figma.getNodeByIdAsync(id);
  if(!node||node.type!=="FRAME"||node.layoutMode!=="HORIZONTAL"||node.width!==1440||![32,112].includes(node.paddingLeft)||![32,112].includes(node.paddingRight))throw new Error("Header no longer matches source shape "+id);
  rows.push(node);
}
for(const [id,blendMode] of grainSpecs){
  const node=await figma.getNodeByIdAsync(id);
  if(!node||node.type!=="FRAME"||!Array.isArray(node.fills)||node.fills.length!==1||node.fills[0].type!=="IMAGE")throw new Error("Missing source grain layer "+id);
  const fill=node.fills[0];
  const original=fill.imageHash&&figma.getImageByHash(fill.imageHash);
  const size=original?await original.getSizeAsync():null;
  if(size?.width!==180||size?.height!==180)throw new Error("Unexpected tile size "+id);
  const {imageTransform,...unchanged}=fill;
  paints.push({node,blendMode,tile:{...unchanged,scaleMode:"TILE",scalingFactor:1}});
}
for(const node of rows){node.paddingLeft=112;node.paddingRight=112;}
for(const {node,blendMode,tile} of paints){node.fills=[tile];node.blendMode=blendMode;}
return {mutatedNodeIds:[...rows.map(n=>n.id),...paints.map(t=>t.node.id)],rows:rows.map(n=>({id:n.id,paddingLeft:n.paddingLeft,paddingRight:n.paddingRight,children:n.children.map(c=>({name:c.name,x:c.x}))})),grain:paints.map(({node})=>({id:node.id,blendMode:node.blendMode,scale:node.fills[0].scaleMode,factor:node.fills[0].scalingFactor,opacity:node.opacity}))};`;
const fontProgram=String.raw`import {chromium} from "playwright-core";
const base=process.argv[1];
const browser=await chromium.launch({executablePath:"/home/ena/.cache/ms-playwright/chromium_headless_shell-1248/chrome-headless-shell-linux64/chrome-headless-shell",headless:true,args:["--disable-webgl"]});
const evidence=[];
try{
 for(const {path,width,height} of [{path:"/",width:1440,height:900},{path:"/",width:390,height:844},{path:"/skills/frontend",width:1440,height:900},{path:"/skills/frontend",width:390,height:844}]){
  const context=await browser.newContext({viewport:{width,height},isMobile:width<500,hasTouch:width<500});
  try{
   const page=await context.newPage();
   await page.goto(base+path,{waitUntil:"commit",timeout:20000});
   await page.locator("main h1").first().waitFor({state:"attached",timeout:35000});
   if(width===390&&path.startsWith("/skills")){
    await page.getByRole("button",{name:"目录",exact:true}).tap();
    await page.locator("#skills-drawer").getByRole("link",{name:"技能文档总览",exact:true}).waitFor();
   }
   await page.evaluate(()=>Promise.race([document.fonts.ready,new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,12000))]));
   const selection=path==="/"?
     [{name:"英文首页标题",query:page.locator("#chapter-skills .reveal-mask span")},{name:"首页中文标题",query:page.locator('section[data-index="0"] p.reveal').filter({hasText:"技能包"}).first()},{name:"首页正文",query:page.locator('section[data-index="0"] p.reveal').filter({hasText:"把一类任务的做法"})}]:
     [{name:"集合标题",query:page.locator("main h1")},{name:"集合正文",query:page.locator("main p").filter({hasText:"界面构建与前端工程相关的技能"}).first()},{name:"目录总览",query:page.locator(width===390?"#skills-drawer":'aside[aria-label="技能目录"]').getByRole("link",{name:"技能文档总览",exact:true})}];
   const nodes=[];
   const client=await context.newCDPSession(page);
   await client.send("DOM.enable");
   await client.send("CSS.enable");
   const {root}=await client.send("DOM.getDocument");
   for(const [ix,item] of selection.entries()){
    const attrs=await item.query.evaluate((node,index)=>{
       node.setAttribute("data-font-qa",String(index));
       const style=getComputedStyle(node);
       return {text:node.textContent.trim().slice(0,35),families:style.fontFamily,weight:style.fontWeight};
    },ix);
    const {nodeId}=await client.send("DOM.querySelector",{nodeId:root.nodeId,selector:'[data-font-qa="'+ix+'"]'});
    const {fonts}=await client.send("CSS.getPlatformFontsForNode",{nodeId});
    nodes.push({name:item.name,...attrs,actual:fonts.filter(f=>f.glyphCount>0).map(f=>f.familyName)});
   }
   await client.detach();
   evidence.push({path,width,height,nodes});
   console.log(JSON.stringify({path,width,height,nodes}));
  }catch(error){console.log(JSON.stringify({path,width,error:error.message.slice(0,350)}));}
  finally{await context.close();}
 }
}finally{await browser.close();}
if(evidence.length!==4)process.exitCode=1;`;
store("t019FontProbeScript",fontProgram);
const q=s=>"'"+s.replace(/'/g,"'\\''")+"'";
const tmp=load("figmaT017Tmp");
const tasks=[
 ["Restore exact header inset and source texture",tools.mcp__figma__use_figma({fileKey:dest,sessionId,skillNames:"resource:figma-use,resource:figma-generate-design",description:"Restore the captured desktop header to the source maximum-width inset and set the original 180-pixel paper grain images to repeating tiles.",code:geometry})],
 ["Measure original font families actually rendered",tools.exec_command({cmd:`mise exec node@24.22.2 -- node --input-type=module -e ${q(fontProgram)} ${q("http://127.0.0.1:"+load("t019Port"))}`,workdir:tmp+"/browser",max_output_tokens:6000,yield_time_ms:1400})]
];
for(const [i,r] of(await Promise.allSettled(tasks.map(([,p])=>p))).entries()){
 if(r.status==="fulfilled")store(i===0?"t019GeometryRepair":"t019SourceFontProbe",r.value);
 text({label:tasks[i][0],result:r});
}

~~~

  "code": "const homes=[\"3:2\",\"4:2\"];\nconst targets=[];\nfor(const id of homes){\n const frame=await figma.getNodeByIdAsync(id);\n if(!frame||frame.type!==\"FRAME\"||frame.parent?.id!==\"0:1\")throw new Error(\"Missing home frame \"+id);\n const texts=frame.findAll(n=>n.type===\"TEXT\"&&n.characters.trim()===\"技能包\");\n if(texts.length!==1)throw new Error(\"Subtitle is ambiguous on \"+id);\n targets.push(texts[0]);\n}\nconst font={family:\"Noto Serif SC\",style:\"Medium\"};\nawait figma.loadFontAsync(font);\nfor(const text of targets)for(const used of text.getRangeAllFontNames(0,text.characters.length))await figma.loadFontAsync(used);\nfor(const text of targets)text.fontName=font;\nreturn {mutatedNodeIds:targets.map(t=>t.id),subtitles:targets.map(t=>({id:t.id,characters:t.characters,font:t.fontName,missing:t.hasMissingFont,width:t.width,height:t.height}))};"

## 工具调用 `exec` · `call_aaaa66d9c7184d75ac690b5c06ec7611`

- 来源定位：字节 7771842

~~~text
工作目录：/home/ena/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init
const sessionId=load("t019Session").structuredContent.sessionId;
const result=await tools.mcp__figma__use_figma({
 fileKey:"xd4v3YLbHr6MFrshieoSPs",sessionId,skillNames:"resource:figma-use,resource:figma-generate-design",
 description:"Restore the original Noto Serif SC face used for the two visible Chinese Skills subtitles after comparing the browser and imported frame.",
 code:`const homes=["3:2","4:2"];
const targets=[];
for(const id of homes){
 const frame=await figma.getNodeByIdAsync(id);
 if(!frame||frame.type!=="FRAME"||frame.parent?.id!=="0:1")throw new Error("Missing home frame "+id);
 const texts=frame.findAll(n=>n.type==="TEXT"&&n.characters.trim()==="技能包");
 if(texts.length!==1)throw new Error("Subtitle is ambiguous on "+id);
 targets.push(texts[0]);
}
const font={family:"Noto Serif SC",style:"Medium"};
await figma.loadFontAsync(font);
for(const text of targets)for(const used of text.getRangeAllFontNames(0,text.characters.length))await figma.loadFontAsync(used);
for(const text of targets)text.fontName=font;
return {mutatedNodeIds:targets.map(t=>t.id),subtitles:targets.map(t=>({id:t.id,characters:t.characters,font:t.fontName,missing:t.hasMissingFont,width:t.width,height:t.height}))};`
});
store("t019SerifRepair",result);
text(result);

~~~

// @exec: {"yield_time_ms": 1000}
const dest="xd4v3YLbHr6MFrshieoSPs";
const sessionId=load("t019Session").structuredContent.sessionId;
const root=load("figmaT017Tmp")+"/t019";
const frames=load("t019Nodes");
const q=s=>"'"+s.replace(/'/g,"'\\''")+"'";
const audit=`const ids=["3:2","4:2","5:2","6:2"];
const out=[];
for(const id of ids){
 const root=await figma.getNodeByIdAsync(id);
 if(!root||root.type!=="FRAME"||root.parent?.id!=="0:1")throw new Error("Unexpected result "+id);
 const text=root.findAll(n=>n.type==="TEXT");
 const adjustables=root.findAll(n=>(n.type==="FRAME"||n.type==="VECTOR")&&typeof n.resize==="function");
 const fonts=text.filter(n=>n.characters==="技能包"||n.characters.startsWith("界面构建")||n.characters.startsWith("把一类任务")).map(n=>({label:n.characters.slice(0,25),fonts:n.getRangeAllFontNames(0,n.characters.length).map(f=>f.family+" "+f.style)}));
 const directory=root.findAll(n=>n.type==="TEXT"&&n.characters==="技能文档总览").length;
 const imgs=root.findAll(n=>n.name==="Canvas"&&Array.isArray(n.fills)&&n.fills.some(p=>p.type==="IMAGE"));
 out.push({id,name:root.name,width:root.width,height:root.height,text:text.length,missing:text.filter(n=>n.hasMissingFont).length,adjustables:adjustables.length,clipsContent:root.clipsContent,fonts,directory,canvasImages:imgs.length});
}
return out;`;
const jobs=[
 ["Verify four named editable frames",async()=>tools.mcp__figma__use_figma({fileKey:dest,sessionId,skillNames:"resource:figma-use",description:"Verify each of the four named results, viewport bounds, editable native text and layout, sampled fonts and canvas image layers.",code:audit})],
 ...frames.map(({key,nodeId})=>[key,async()=>{
    const s=await tools.mcp__figma__get_screenshot({fileKey:dest,nodeId,sessionId,maxDimension:1440});
    const payload=s.content.find(x=>x.type==="text")?.text;
    if(!payload)throw new Error("Missing screenshot response");
    const head=JSON.parse(payload);
    if(!head.image_url?.startsWith("https://"))throw new Error("Unexpected screenshot URL");
    const d=await tools.exec_command({cmd:`curl --fail --silent --show-error --location --retry 2 --retry-delay 1 --retry-all-errors --connect-timeout 6 --max-time 18 --output ${q(key+"-review.png")} ${q(head.image_url)} && sha256sum ${q(key+"-review.png")}`,workdir:root,max_output_tokens:450,yield_time_ms:1500});
    return {width:head.width,height:head.height,...d};
 }])
];
for(const [i,r] of(await Promise.allSettled(jobs.map(([,fn])=>fn()))).entries()){
 if(r.status==="fulfilled")store(i===0?"t019FinalStructural":"t019ReviewFile-"+jobs[i][0],r.value);
 text({label:jobs[i][0],result:r});
}

{

2026-11-27 使用 T017 的 Make 原型快照和 T018 的采集方式。一次 Playwright 主流程依次启动四个样例，为每项设置实际视口并核对章节或目录状态，调用 Figma 采集脚本的 \`captureOnceForDesign\`。四次提交均返回 HTTP 200，四个采集编号均取得同一 Design 文件的完成回执。布局调整和命名在四块原生画板上以 Figma 脚本执行。 [实施方案](../implementation/T019.md) · [目标页面 Page 1](https://www.figma.com/design/${fileKey}/Untitled?node-id=0-1)

### 实际画板

将四块画板的 Figma 渲染图逐项与同一次浏览器采集保存的参考图对照。各画板均为 \`Page 1\` 下的原生 FRAME，画板间无重叠。常规文字为有 \`characters\` 的 TEXT 层，导航和布局含可选择、可调整的原生框架与矢量层。

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
${table}

### 差异与处理

- 两块桌面画板导入时忽略了顶部容器的居中最大宽度，品牌图标的左边距为 32 像素。按源预览恢复左右各 112 像素，复核了主导航的居中位置。
- 首页纸纹导入时只绘制了左上角的单个纹理块。保留原采集的 180 × 180 像素纹理，恢复图像平铺、原透明度和原混合方式，复核了两个视口的完整纹理。
- 手机集合页导入时按完整文档定高 861 像素。以源视口 390 × 844 裁切，复核了展开抽屉、选中态和遮罩。
- 浏览器在两块首页源画面均为实时 Three.js，Figma 保留了当时的渲染图像。画板中的文件夹是图像层，旋转和章节入场动画在原型中查看。浏览器参考图与导入静帧之间可见连续动画带来的轻微姿态差异。
- 英文首页标题实际使用 \`Instrument Serif\`，品牌及集合标题使用 \`Geist\`，导入文字保留这些字体。首页中文标题的源浏览器使用 \`Noto Serif SC\`，导入时附在 \`Instrument Serif\` 的回退字形上。已将两处标题的原生文字恢复为 \`Noto Serif SC Medium\`（字重 500），并核对了衬线字形。四块画板的 Figma 字体缺失计数均为 0。
- 源浏览器的中文正文和目录实际使用 \`Source Han Sans CN\`，导入文字的无衬线中文使用 Figma 回退字形，首页正文可见换行差异。已核对目标可用字体清单，当前可用 \`Noto Sans SC\`。是否明确采用该字体替代中文无衬线字形，已提交给设计协作者确认。

| 检查 | 结果 | 证据或恢复条件 |
| --- | --- | --- |

| 字体替代 | 待确认 | 当前 Design 的可用字体中没有 \`Source Han Sans CN\`。设计协作者确认 \`Noto Sans SC\` 后，可按确认范围处理并复核。若要求精确匹配，则需让目标文件具备原字体后再检查 |

`;

const cwd="/home/ena/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init";
const todo="specs/docs/001-docs-site-framework/tasks.md";
const result=await tools.exec_command({cmd:`rg '^[-] \\[ \\] T019 \\[US6\\] ' ${todo}`,workdir:cwd,max_output_tokens:850});
const line=result.output.trim();
if(result.exit_code!==0||!line.includes("[实施方案](implementation/T019.md)")||line.includes("\n")||line.includes("t019-代表性试转"))throw new Error("T019 reference differs from the reviewed task");
text(await tools.apply_patch(`*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: ${todo}\n@@\n-${line}\n+${line} · [代表性试转记录](analysis/figma-conversion.md#t019-代表性试转)\n*** End Patch`));
const verify=String.raw`python3 - <<'PY'
import re
from pathlib import Path
report=Path("specs/docs/001-docs-site-framework/analysis/figma-conversion.md")
t019=report.read_text().split("## T019 代表性试转\n",1)[1]
links=re.findall(r"https://www\.figma\.com/design/xd4v3YLbHr6MFrshieoSPs/Untitled\?node-id=([3-6]-2)",t019)
assert links == ["3-2","4-2","5-2","6-2"]
task=Path("specs/docs/001-docs-site-framework/tasks.md").read_text()
assert re.search(r"^- \[ \] T019 .+analysis/figma-conversion.md#t019-代表性试转",task,re.M)
assert re.search(r"^- \[ \] T020 ",task,re.M)
assert (report.parent / "../implementation/T019.md").is_file()
print("Confirmed four frame links and the pending T019 acceptance recorded")
PY`;
const jobs=[
 ["Format and verify T019 evidence",tools.exec_command({cmd:`pnpm exec eslint --fix --no-ignore --max-warnings 0 specs/docs/001-docs-site-framework/{analysis/figma-conversion.md,implementation/T019.md,tasks.md} && git diff --check && ${verify}`,workdir:cwd,max_output_tokens:950})],
 ["Stop temporary preview",tools.write_stdin({session_id:load("t019Preview").session_id,chars:"\u0003",yield_time_ms:1000,max_output_tokens:500})]
];
for(const [i,r] of(await Promise.allSettled(jobs.map(([,p])=>p))).entries())text({label:jobs[i][0],result:r});
